# The journey as a text adventure (learner child)

Everything the game said and everything the child did, level by level, in play order. Times are game seconds.

## Ninja Training (tutorial)

   0.0s SENSEI: Ninja training! First, tap the big gong!
   0.1s   --- tut-gong ---
   0.2s   > child taps: gong
   1.8s   [gong]
   1.8s SENSEI: Stuck? Tap me, down here in the corner. Try it now!
   2.6s   --- tut-help ---
   2.6s   [great]
   2.6s   > child taps: Help
   2.6s SENSEI: That's it! I'm always here to help.
   5.0s   > child taps: Help
   5.2s SENSEI: And when you tap the speaker, I'll say it again!
   6.2s   --- tut-speaker ---
   6.3s   [good]
   6.3s   > child taps: Hear it again
   6.3s SENSEI: And when you tap the speaker, I'll say it again!
   8.7s   > child taps: Hear it again
   9.2s SENSEI: Welcome to Bamboo Village!
  11.3s SENSEI: Tap the glowing stone to start your next adventure.

## Show Sensei (placement)

   0.0s SENSEI: Let's see what you know! Just have a go.
   0.2s   --- find ---
   0.2s   > child taps: o
   1.4s   [good]
   2.2s SENSEI: Which is the spelling of this sound?
   2.2s SENSEI: Which is the spelling of this sound?
   2.7s   [wrong]
   2.7s   > child taps: i
   3.4s   [fanfare]
   3.4s SENSEI: Hooray! We'll start right at the beginning.
   4.3s   [great]
   6.1s SENSEI: Welcome to Bamboo Village!
   8.2s SENSEI: Tap the glowing stone to start your next adventure.

## Bamboo Village w1-wu1: ears

   0.0s SENSEI: Ninja ears on! Let's listen to some words.
   0.3s   --- warmup ---
   3.3s SENSEI: This is the sun.
   5.2s SENSEI: This is a sock.
   6.7s SENSEI: This is a cat.
   8.1s SENSEI: Let me show you!
   9.3s SENSEI: Tap the sun!
  10.9s   [good]
  11.8s SENSEI: Now you try!
  12.7s SENSEI: Tap the sock!
  13.6s   > child taps: sock
  13.7s   [good]
  14.5s SENSEI: Watch me first!
  15.9s SENSEI: I can say a word fast. Sun!
  17.9s SENSEI: Or I can say it slowly...
      🔊 "sun" (slowly)
  22.3s SENSEI: Fast or slow, it's the same word. Sun!
  25.6s SENSEI: When I say a word slowly, I can hear the sounds that make up the word. Words are made of sounds!
  32.8s SENSEI: Your turn! Tap the tortoise, and say it slowly with me.
  33.1s   > child taps: tortoise
      🔊 "sun" (slowly)
  36.0s SENSEI: Now tap the rabbit, and say it fast!
  36.7s   > child taps: sun
      🔊 "sun" (slowly)
  39.2s SENSEI: Now tap the rabbit, and say it fast!
  41.5s   > child taps: rabbit
      🔊 "sun"
  43.1s SENSEI: Listen to the very first sound.
      🔊 "sun" (first sound held) · "sock" (first sound held)
  48.2s SENSEI: Did you notice? Sun and sock start with the same sound...
      🔊 /s/
  54.0s SENSEI: Say that sound with me!
      🔊 /s/
  58.6s SENSEI: This is a sausage.
  60.8s SENSEI: This is the moon.
  62.7s SENSEI: Let me show you!
  63.8s SENSEI: Tap all the pictures that start with...
      🔊 /s/
  68.3s   [good]
      🔊 "sun" (first sound held)
  69.5s SENSEI: Your turn!
  70.7s   > child taps: sock
  70.7s   [good]
      🔊 "sock" (first sound held)
  72.1s SENSEI: Three right answers in a row! Your ninja is getting stronger.
  76.8s   > child taps: sun
  80.4s   > child taps: sausage
  80.4s   [good]
      🔊 "sausage" (first sound held)
  82.3s SENSEI: You found them both! They both start with...
      🔊 /s/
  87.0s   [great]
  87.0s SENSEI: You can hear the sounds in words. Brilliant listening!
  88.4s   [great]
  90.7s   [gong]
  90.8s SENSEI: Look! Your pictures are turning into stickers!
  91.3s   --- stickers ---
  94.6s SENSEI: This is your Sticker Book!
  96.0s SENSEI: Sun, sock, cat, sausage and moon!
 101.0s SENSEI: Every picture you play with becomes a sticker!
 103.7s SENSEI: Tap a sticker!
 104.6s   > child taps: sticker sun
      🔊 "sun" · "sun" (slowly) · "sun" · "sun" (slowly)
 107.0s SENSEI: Tap the green arrow to carry on!
